**FINDING 1 (docs/config-accuracy, daytrading min_confidence actually 0.7 not 0.8):** Source audit + live skip evidence prove daytrading enforces **0.7** (FALLBACK_CONFIG.minConfidence=0.7, src/index.ts:125), NOT the 0.8 asserted in prior controls. Root cause: `resolveDaytradingConfig` (src/index.ts:153) applies a D1 bot_config key only `if (key in FALLBACK_CONFIG)` where FALLBACK_CONFIG uses camelCase (`minConfidence`), but D1 stores snake_case `min_confidence` (bot_config row = 0.8, displayed live in /api/config). So the snake_case D1 `min_confidence`=0.8 is **never applied** to the daytrading risk manager; the daytrading entry gate uses the 0.7 FALLBACK default. Live proof: daytrading NO_ENTRY_RISK skips on runs 9585/9587 report "Confidence 0.68 below minimum 0.7". All prior C-464..C-490 records' "min_conf 0.8 live" claims are therefore inaccurate for daytrading (the woven same-snake-mismatch also silently ignores swing_* snake_case keys but swing fallback 0.5 == intended so no divergence). NOTE: wiring 0.8 in (config-key normalization) would RAISE the daytrading entry threshold → a risk-parameter-like trading-behavior change → REQUIRES Joachim's explicit decision on intended daytrading min_confidence (0.7 vs 0.8) BEFORE any code change/deploy. NO deploy made this control. Follow-up added: decide+wire daytrading min_confidence.

**FINDING 2 (run-log observability, trades_executed lags same-cycle async fills):** Daytrading cycles that placed today's open fills (runs 9585/9588/9590) log `trades_executed: 0` and status "skipped" even though the fills are broker-confirmed and attributed (trade rows 1181-1187 filled). Root cause: `tradesExecuted` increments only `if (alpaca.isOrderFullyFilled(order))` at cycle time (src/index.ts:1216); when broker fill confirmation lags logRun (order still settling), the run row records exec 0 but the trade is later persisted filled via the broker sync/reconciliation path. NOT a data-loss: positions/trades are broker-authoritative and complete. Pre-existing async-timing characteristic (Sep 8 run 9104 logged exec 1 on a 13:47 fill; today's 9585 at same time logged 0: timing-dependent, not a clean 2.7.0 regression). Monitored, no code change.

**VERDICT: HEALTHY operationally (all schedules, positions, equity, no 12th dispatch); two documented findings (daytrading min_confidence doc-vs-live 0.7/0.8 wiring gap; run-log trades_executed async lag). No deploy (both are observability/docs accuracy, trading-behavior change would need Joachim's decision).**

## Control-465 (Sep 8 ~19:00 UTC, ~21:00 CEST): accounting diagnosis — corruption theory DISPROVEN, FIFO economics computable

Direct D1 audit of the trades table disproved the corrupt-filled_notional theory: filled_notional is not a stored column at all (the API computes it from filled_qty x avg_fill_price), and legacy rows are internally consistent. The real story: the early uncapped era (Aug 3-4) traded genuine $20k-$60k single positions (e.g. F buys of 1,347/1,336/1,338 shares, ~$60k total, strategy NULL, before strategy attribution and before capital caps existed).

FIFO realized P&L reconstructed from all 1,128 filled trades: **-$1,935.03 total** (2026-08: -$1,257.87; 2026-09 so far: -$677.16). Worst symbols: CCL -$519.92, FCEL -$433.26, F -$430.30, NIO -$217.00, NKE -$207.33, VZ -$197.17. Best: MSTR +$351.06, NCLH +$235.21, META +$137.40. Combined with $269.11 crypto fees, ~$3.71 regulatory, and ~-$37 unrealized on the open book, this is consistent with the ~-$2,328 all-time equity drop. The money went to broad realized losses across many symbols, concentrated in the early uncapped August era, plus crypto fees.

Remaining observability gap (accurate scope): the trade-ledger open FIFO basis is $41,958 vs ~$10,356 broker cost basis of the open book — buys exist in the ledger whose closes were not recorded as sell trades (position closes outside the trade path), so the FIFO realized figure is a least-negative estimate and per-sell gross attribution is still not durable. Release 2.7.x follow-up: FIFO lot-matching in the write path + maintenance backfill of per-sell gross (fees stay conservative/unattributed).

## Release 2.7.0 + Control-464 (Sep 8 16:29 UTC, ~18:29 CEST): USD entry risk guards deployed live after Joachim-approved bleed-stop package

CONTEXT: Joachim flagged Sep 8 that the account has only lost money since start (~-$2,328 all-time, $100,000 -> ~$97,672) and required the general development to stop being negative. Diagnosis from live data: crypto net -325.73 USD (incl. 269 fees, 1 win / 8 losses), daytrading win rate 30.8% (7.1% overall), swing 0 wins in the tracked window, and ~$1.9k realized losses concentrated in the early uncapped August era (FIFO on the trade ledger: Aug -$1,258, Sep -$677 so far; worst symbols CCL/FCEL/F/NIO/NKE/VZ; plus $269 crypto fees; consistent with the equity drop). Root cause of the bleed: the legacy risk limits are account-level percentages (daily_loss_limit_pct 15 = ~$14,500 on this account; rolling 10% over a ~100-minute window) that can never trip intraday, so an unprofitable strategy can run indefinitely.

DEPLOYED (commit 2fe9c5a, direct CF API PUT /content 200 + schedules re-registered 200):
- src/risk-guards.ts: DAILY_LOSS_LIMIT_USD (daytrading 150 / swing 100 / crypto 100; strategy daily P&L = positions closed today UTC + broker intraday unrealized) and ACCOUNT_DRAWDOWN_FLOOR (97,000 USD absolute). Blocked guards pause new BUY entries only; exits, reconciliation, caps untouched. Fail closed on evaluation error (RISK_GUARD_UNAVAILABLE). Blank/malformed/negative overrides keep defaults; explicit 0 disables.
- Wired into daytrading (index.ts), swing (swing-strategy.ts), crypto (crypto-strategy.ts) with cycle- and decision-level structured skips.
- Crypto formally disabled: CRYPTO_DISABLED_BY_CONFIG fail-closed gate (only explicit 'true' re-enables, after fee telemetry freshness + backtested edge). Flat book ends the cycle with an auditable skip instead of opaque CRYPTO_BARS_STALE.
- Daytrading min_confidence raised 0.7 -> 0.8 (churn/edge bar).
- D1 config written via D1 REST (7 changes): daily_loss_limit_usd 150, swing 100, crypto 100, account_equity_floor_usd 97000, crypto_trading_enabled false, version 2.7.0, min_confidence 0.8.
- Version 2.7.0 aligned across version.ts, package.json, schema.sql seed, dashboard footer, release test.

VERIFIED LIVE (GET-only): /health ok 2.7.0; /api/config shows all guard keys + min_confidence 0.8. Caps unchanged 5000/3700/2000 (vital parameters untouched). 241 tests / 893 assertions, typecheck clean.

FOLLOW-UPS: (1) observe first live guard evaluations on next cron fires (expect no RISK_GUARD_UNAVAILABLE, crypto run logged skipped CRYPTO_DISABLED_BY_CONFIG); (2) accounting repair of corrupt legacy filled_notional + FIFO realized P&L per fill (release 2.7.x) so losses are precisely attributable; (3) equity is $672 above the 97,000 floor — if the floor trips, all new entries halt pending Joachim review by design; (4) swing fires tonight 22:00 UTC DOW=3 under the new guard.
